Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in algorithms theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 107 and the invariant I(A,B) = 654 mod 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 107 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the algorithms structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 654. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
